more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Ceht75AvxneXw3AS1s2En","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018hiMqaavcWCdbXkSWFn127","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed-Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization: the expensive part of the algorithm is the evaluation of\n// every remaining unclustered point as a candidate seed, which is independent\n// across seeds. Those seed evaluations are distributed cyclically over all MPI\n// ranks; a single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) then selects the globally best\n// candidate cluster (largest cardinality, lowest point index on ties, exactly\n// as the sequential code does) and its owner broadcasts the member list so\n// that every rank keeps an identical view of the clustering state.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\nstatic int g_rank = 0;\nstatic int g_nranks = 1;\n\n// Only rank 0 produces program output\n#define RPRINTF(...)                       \\\n    do {                                   \\\n        if (g_rank == 0) printf(__VA_ARGS__); \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Compact, structure-of-arrays view of the points that are still unclustered.\n// It is rebuilt (identically) on every rank once per outer iteration.\nstruct PointSet {\n    std::vector<double> x, y;\n    std::vector<int> index;  // original index of each entry\n};\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over a PointSet. The cell size is never smaller than the\n// clustering threshold, so every point within the threshold of a given point is\n// contained in the 3x3 block of cells around that point's own cell.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    double inv_cell = 1.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;  // nx*ny+1 offsets into items\n    std::vector<int> items;  // entry ids, ascending within each cell\n\n    inline int cellX(const double px) const {\n        return std::min(nx - 1, static_cast<int>(px * inv_cell));\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const double py) const {\n        return std::min(ny - 1, static_cast<int>(py * inv_cell));\n    }\n};\n\n// Reusable buffers for the candidate list of a single seed\nstruct Scratch {\n    std::vector<int> id;         // entry ids of the candidates\n    std::vector<double> x, y;    // candidate coordinates\n    std::vector<double> maxd;    // max distance from candidate to cluster so far\n};\n\nvoid buildGrid(const PointSet& ps, const double threshold, Grid& g) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(ps.index.size());\n\n    // Keep the number of cells proportional to the number of points, while\n    // still covering the threshold radius with a 3x3 block of cells.\n    const double min_cell = std::sqrt(MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT / std::max(1, n));\n    const double cell = std::max(threshold, min_cell);\n\n    g.inv_cell = 1.0 / cell;\n    g.nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH * g.inv_cell) + 1;\n    g.ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT * g.inv_cell) + 1;\n\n    const int ncells = g.nx * g.ny;\n    g.start.assign(ncells + 1, 0);\n    g.items.resize(n);\n\n    // Counting sort of the entries into their cells\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        ++g.start[g.cellY(ps.y[i]) * g.nx + g.cellX(ps.x[i]) + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n        g.start[c + 1] += g.start[c];\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cursor(g.start.begin(), g.start.end() - 1);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        g.items[cursor[g.cellY(ps.y[i]) * g.nx + g.cellX(ps.x[i])]++] = i;\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from the seed entry `seed` of `ps`, using the same\n// greedy rule as the sequential reference: repeatedly add the unclustered point\n// whose resulting maximum distance to all current members is minimal and below\n// the threshold (ties broken towards the lowest point index).\n//\n// Two exact optimizations are applied:\n//   * only points closer than the threshold to the seed can ever be added,\n//     since the seed itself is a member; the grid supplies them directly.\n//   * the maximum distance of every candidate to the current members is\n//     maintained incrementally instead of being recomputed from scratch.\n//\n// `members` receives the original point indices in insertion order.\n// Returns the cardinality of the candidate cluster.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed,\n                             const PointSet& ps,\n                             const Grid& g,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             std::vector<int>& members,\n                             Scratch& scr) {\n    const double sx = ps.x[seed];\n    const double sy = ps.y[seed];\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(ps.index[seed]);\n\n    // Collect the candidates: unclustered points within the threshold of the seed\n    scr.id.clear();\n    const int cx0 = g.cellX(sx);\n    const int cy0 = g.cellY(sy);\n    const int cx_lo = std::max(0, cx0 - 1), cx_hi = std::min(g.nx - 1, cx0 + 1);\n    const int cy_lo = std::max(0, cy0 - 1), cy_hi = std::min(g.ny - 1, cy0 + 1);\n\n    for (int cy = cy_lo; cy <= cy_hi; ++cy) {\n        for (int cx = cx_lo; cx <= cx_hi; ++cx) {\n            const int c = cy * g.nx + cx;\n            const int end = g.start[c + 1];\n            for (int k = g.start[c]; k < end; ++k) {\n                const int t = g.items[k];\n                if (t == seed) continue;\n                const double dx = ps.x[t] - sx;\n                const double dy = ps.y[t] - sy;\n                if (std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < threshold) {\n                    scr.id.push_back(t);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Ascending entry ids reproduce the candidate scan order of the reference\n    std::sort(scr.id.begin(), scr.id.end());\n\n    const int nc = static_cast<int>(scr.id.size());\n    scr.x.resize(nc);\n    scr.y.resize(nc);\n    scr.maxd.resize(nc);\n    for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n        const int t = scr.id[i];\n        const double dx = ps.x[t] - sx;\n        const double dy = ps.y[t] - sy;\n        scr.x[i] = ps.x[t];\n        scr.y[i] = ps.y[t];\n        scr.maxd[i] = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n\n    const double* __restrict cand_x = scr.x.data();\n    const double* __restrict cand_y = scr.y.data();\n    double* __restrict maxd = scr.maxd.data();\n    const double kInf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    for (;;) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        double best = threshold;\n        int best_i = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n            if (maxd[i] < best) {\n                best = maxd[i];\n                best_i = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_i < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n        members.push_back(ps.index[scr.id[best_i]]);\n\n        // Members are excluded from further consideration via an infinite bound\n        const double px = cand_x[best_i];\n        const double py = cand_y[best_i];\n        maxd[best_i] = kInf;\n        for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n            const double dx = cand_x[i] - px;\n            const double dy = cand_y[i] - py;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            maxd[i] = std::max(maxd[i], d);\n        }\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI ranks)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    PointSet ps;\n    Grid grid;\n    Scratch scr;\n    std::vector<int> best_members, candidate_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Rebuild the compact set of unclustered points and its lookup grid.\n        // This is replicated work, but only O(N) per cluster found.\n        ps.x.clear();\n        ps.y.clear();\n        ps.index.clear();\n        ps.x.reserve(remaining);\n        ps.y.reserve(remaining);\n        ps.index.reserve(remaining);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            if (!clustered[i]) {\n                ps.x.push_back(points[i].x);\n                ps.y.push_back(points[i].y);\n                ps.index.push_back(i);\n            }\n        }\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(ps.index.size());\n        buildGrid(ps, threshold, grid);\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed; seeds are spread cyclically\n        // across the ranks, which balances the varying per-seed cost well\n        // because consecutive indices are spatially correlated.\n        int local_best[2] = {-1, -1};  // {cardinality, seed entry id}\n        best_members.clear();\n        for (int seed = g_rank; seed < U; seed += g_nranks) {\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, ps, grid, threshold,\n                                                             candidate_members, scr);\n            if (cardinality > local_best[0]) {\n                local_best[0] = cardinality;\n                local_best[1] = seed;\n                best_members.swap(candidate_members);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Largest cardinality wins; MPI_MAXLOC breaks ties towards the smallest\n        // entry id, i.e. the lowest point index, matching the sequential order.\n        int global_best[2];\n        MPI_Allreduce(local_best, global_best, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (global_best[0] <= 0) break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        const int cardinality = global_best[0];\n        const int seed = global_best[1];\n        const int owner = seed % g_nranks;\n\n        // The owner's local best is exactly the global best: it retained the\n        // lowest-id seed of maximal cardinality among its own seeds.\n        if (g_rank != owner) {\n            best_members.assign(cardinality, 0);\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(best_members.data(), cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = ps.index[seed];\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (int member : clusters.back().members) {\n            clustered[member] = 1;\n        }\n        remaining -= cardinality;\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    RPRINTF(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    RPRINTF(\"Options:\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            RPRINTF(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        RPRINTF(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n               num_points, threshold);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    RPRINTF(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    RPRINTF(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    RPRINTF(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Generate synthetic data. The generator is cheap and deterministic, so\n    // every rank replicates it instead of broadcasting the point set.\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    RPRINTF(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    RPRINTF(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    RPRINTF(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n           total_clustered, num_points,\n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    RPRINTF(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    RPRINTF(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    RPRINTF(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && g_rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        // The clustering state is replicated, so rank 0 validates and shares\n        // the verdict to keep the exit code consistent across ranks.\n        int valid = 1;\n        if (g_rank == 0) {\n            valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold) ? 1 : 0;\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            RPRINTF(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            RPRINTF(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":322,"cache_read_input_tokens":32075,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":322},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3dec33f-3d14-4be1-8623-3ee82386655f","uuid":"c30af986-972a-42f3-ac30-01642af9247c","timestamp":"2026-09-04T05:56:41.082Z","request_id":"req_011Ceht74NK1XkjXXAVtTdmV"}

Those seed evaluations are distributed cyclically over all MPI\n// ranks; a single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) then selects the globally best\n// candidate cluster (largest cardinality, lowest point index on ties, exactly\n// as the sequential code does) and its owner broadcasts the member list so\n// that every rank keeps an identical view of the clustering state.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\nstatic int g_rank = 0;\nstatic int g_nranks = 1;\n\n// Only rank 0 produces program output\n#define RPRINTF(...)                       \\\n    do {                                   \\\n        if (g_rank == 0) printf(__VA_ARGS__); \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Compact, structure-of-arrays view of the points that are still unclustered.\n// It is rebuilt (identically) on every rank once per outer iteration.\nstruct PointSet {\n    std::vector<double> x, y;\n    std::vector<int> index;  // original index of each entry\n};\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over a PointSet. The cell size is never smaller than the\n// clustering threshold, so every point within the threshold of a given point is\n// contained in the 3x3 block of cells around that point's own cell.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    double inv_cell = 1.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;  // nx*ny+1 offsets into items\n    std::vector<int> items;  // entry ids, ascending within each cell\n\n    inline int cellX(const double px) const {\n        return std::min(nx - 1, static_cast<int>(px * inv_cell));\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const double py) const {\n        return std::min(ny - 1, static_cast<int>(py * inv_cell));\n    }\n};\n\n// Reusable buffers for the candidate list of a single seed\nstruct Scratch {\n    std::vector<int> id;         // entry ids of the candidates\n    std::vector<double> x, y;    // candidate coordinates\n    std::vector<double> maxd;    // max distance from candidate to cluster so far\n};\n\nvoid buildGrid(const PointSet& ps, const double threshold, Grid& g) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(ps.index.size());\n\n    // Keep the number of cells proportional to the number of points, while\n    // still covering the threshold radius with a 3x3 block of cells.\n    const double min_cell = std::sqrt(MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT / std::max(1, n));\n    const double cell = std::max(threshold, min_cell);\n\n    g.inv_cell = 1.0 / cell;\n    g.nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH * g.inv_cell) + 1;\n    g.ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT * g.inv_cell) + 1;\n\n    const int ncells = g.nx * g.ny;\n    g.start.assign(ncells + 1, 0);\n    g.items.resize(n);\n\n    // Counting sort of the entries into their cells\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        ++g.start[g.cellY(ps.y[i]) * g.nx + g.cellX(ps.x[i]) + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n        g.start[c + 1] += g.start[c];\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cursor(g.start.begin(), g.start.end() - 1);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        g.items[cursor[g.cellY(ps.y[i]) * g.nx + g.cellX(ps.x[i])]++] = i;\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from the seed entry `seed` of `ps`, using the same\n// greedy rule as the sequential reference: repeatedly add the unclustered point\n// whose resulting maximum distance to all current members is minimal and below\n// the threshold (ties broken towards the lowest point index).\n//\n// Two exact optimizations are applied:\n//   * only points closer than the threshold to the seed can ever be added,\n//     since the seed itself is a member; the grid supplies them directly.\n//   * the maximum distance of every candidate to the current members is\n//     maintained incrementally instead of being recomputed from scratch.\n//\n// `members` receives the original point indices in insertion order.\n// Returns the cardinality of the candidate cluster.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed,\n                             const PointSet& ps,\n                             const Grid& g,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             std::vector<int>& members,\n                             Scratch& scr) {\n    const double sx = ps.x[seed];\n    const double sy = ps.y[seed];\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(ps.index[seed]);\n\n    // Collect the candidates: unclustered points within the threshold of the seed\n    scr.id.clear();\n    const int cx0 = g.cellX(sx);\n    const int cy0 = g.cellY(sy);\n    const int cx_lo = std::max(0, cx0 - 1), cx_hi = std::min(g.nx - 1, cx0 + 1);\n    const int cy_lo = std::max(0, cy0 - 1), cy_hi = std::min(g.ny - 1, cy0 + 1);\n\n    for (int cy = cy_lo; cy <= cy_hi; ++cy) {\n        for (int cx = cx_lo; cx <= cx_hi; ++cx) {\n            const int c = cy * g.nx + cx;\n            const int end = g.start[c + 1];\n            for (int k = g.start[c]; k < end; ++k) {\n                const int t = g.items[k];\n                if (t == seed) continue;\n                const double dx = ps.x[t] - sx;\n                const double dy = ps.y[t] - sy;\n                if (std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < threshold) {\n                    scr.id.push_back(t);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Ascending entry ids reproduce the candidate scan order of the reference\n    std::sort(scr.id.begin(), scr.id.end());\n\n    const int nc = static_cast<int>(scr.id.size());\n    scr.x.resize(nc);\n    scr.y.resize(nc);\n    scr.maxd.resize(nc);\n    for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n        const int t = scr.id[i];\n        const double dx = ps.x[t] - sx;\n        const double dy = ps.y[t] - sy;\n        scr.x[i] = ps.x[t];\n        scr.y[i] = ps.y[t];\n        scr.maxd[i] = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n\n    const double* __restrict cand_x = scr.x.data();\n    const double* __restrict cand_y = scr.y.data();\n    double* __restrict maxd = scr.maxd.data();\n    const double kInf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    for (;;) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        double best = threshold;\n        int best_i = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n            if (maxd[i] < best) {\n                best = maxd[i];\n                best_i = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_i < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n        members.push_back(ps.index[scr.id[best_i]]);\n\n        // Members are excluded from further consideration via an infinite bound\n        const double px = cand_x[best_i];\n        const double py = cand_y[best_i];\n        maxd[best_i] = kInf;\n        for (int i = 0; i < nc; ++i) {\n            const double dx = cand_x[i] - px;\n            const double dy = cand_y[i] - py;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            maxd[i] = std::max(maxd[i], d);\n        }\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI ranks)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    PointSet ps;\n    Grid grid;\n    Scratch scr;\n    std::vector<int> best_members, candidate_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Rebuild the compact set of unclustered points and its lookup grid.\n        // This is replicated work, but only O(N) per cluster found.\n        ps.x.clear();\n        ps.y.clear();\n        ps.index.clear();\n        ps.x.reserve(remaining);\n        ps.y.reserve(remaining);\n        ps.index.reserve(remaining);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            if (!clustered[i]) {\n                ps.x.push_back(points[i].x);\n                ps.y.push_back(points[i].y);\n                ps.index.push_back(i);\n            }\n        }\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(ps.index.size());\n        buildGrid(ps, threshold, grid);\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed; seeds are spread cyclically\n        // across the ranks, which balances the varying per-seed cost well\n        // because consecutive indices are spatially correlated.\n        int local_best[2] = {-1, -1};  // {cardinality, seed entry id}\n        best_members.clear();\n        for (int seed = g_rank; seed < U; seed += g_nranks) {\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, ps, grid, threshold,\n                                                             candidate_members, scr);\n            if (cardinality > local_best[0]) {\n                local_best[0] = cardinality;\n                local_best[1] = seed;\n                best_members.swap(candidate_members);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Largest cardinality wins; MPI_MAXLOC breaks ties towards the smallest\n        // entry id, i.e. the lowest point index, matching the sequential order.\n        int global_best[2];\n        MPI_Allreduce(local_best, global_best, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (global_best[0] <= 0) break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        const int cardinality = global_best[0];\n        const int seed = global_best[1];\n        const int owner = seed % g_nranks;\n\n        // The owner's local best is exactly the global best: it retained the\n        // lowest-id seed of maximal cardinality among its own seeds.\n        if (g_rank != owner) {\n            best_members.assign(cardinality, 0);\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(best_members.data(), cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = ps.index[seed];\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (int member : clusters.back().members) {\n            clustered[member] = 1;\n        }\n        remaining -= cardinality;\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    RPRINTF(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    RPRINTF(\"Options:\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            RPRINTF(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        RPRINTF(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n               num_points, threshold);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    RPRINTF(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    RPRINTF(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    RPRINTF(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    RPRINTF(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Generate synthetic data. }"]}],"originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CehubBpgY6vmJHvEQe3co","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp` and `qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt` are modified in place; no new files, same executable name, MPI unconditionally.\n\n## What the parallelization does\n\nThe outer loop of QT clustering is inherently sequential (each round removes the winning cluster), but within a round every unclustered point is evaluated as a candidate seed independently — that's where all the time goes.\n\n- **Seed distribution**: seeds are handed out cyclically (`seed = rank; seed += nranks`). Consecutive point indices are spatially correlated in the generator, so cyclic assignment mixes cheap (sparse) and expensive (dense) seeds evenly across ranks.\n- **Winner selection**: one `MPI_Allreduce` with `MPI_2INT`/`MPI_MAXLOC` on `{cardinality, seed}`. `MAXLOC` breaks ties towards the smallest index, which reproduces the reference's \"first seed with strictly greater cardinality wins\" rule exactly. The owning rank's local best is provably the global best, so a single `MPI_Bcast` of the member list follows — two collectives per cluster found, nothing else.\n- **Replicated state**: the point set is generated identically on every rank (cheap, deterministic) and all ranks apply the same membership update, so no state ever needs gathering; rank 0 alone prints and validates, with the verdict broadcast so exit codes agree.\n\n## Two exact serial optimizations\n\nBoth preserve bit-identical results (no switch to squared distances, which could shift a tie):\n\n1. **Neighborhood restriction** — since the seed is always a member, a point at distance ≥ threshold from the seed can never be added. A uniform bucket grid (cell size ≥ threshold, cell count kept ∝ N) yields exactly those candidates from a 3×3 cell block.\n2. **Incremental max-distance** — instead of recomputing each candidate's max distance to all members every iteration, the per-candidate bound is updated against only the newly added member (max is order-independent, so this is exact). Reduces cluster growth from O(k²·N) to O(k·m).\n\n## Measured results\n\nVerified bit-identical `Hash`/`Sum`/cluster output vs. the original binary for `-n 500 -t 3`, `-n 1000 -t 2`, `-n 3000 -t 2`, `-n 8000 -t 2`, `-n 1200 -t 0.7`, `-n 300 -t 0.05`, `-n 200 -t 50`, at 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 8 and 64 ranks.\n\n| n=8000, t=2.0 | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| time (ms) | 10495 | 5500 | 2680 | 1375 | 736 | 564 | 307 | 162 |\n| speedup | 1.0 | 1.9 | 3.9 | 7.6 | 14.3 | 18.6 | 34.2 | 64.8 |\n\n(The 32-rank point is placement noise on this 256-core box; the trend either side is near-linear.) Absolute effect including the serial optimizations, `-n 3000 -t 2`: 478 s → 0.128 s at 8 ranks.\n\nOne pre-existing bug, unrelated to these changes and left as is: `generateSyntheticData` loops forever for `n < ~30`, because `group_cnt = frand() * (N/30.0)` truncates to 0 and no points are ever emitted. The unmodified original hangs identically on `-n 1`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":332,"cache_read_input_tokens":50531,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":332},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3dec33f-3d14-4be1-8623-3ee82386655f","uuid":"fe66c8e1-7e8a-4f29-9b79-59c0b53e8421","timestamp":"2026-09-04T06:14:38.474Z","request_id":"req_011CehubAtsTt4u2dmHqPW6q"}
